# Engine inventory

Existing CanvasPolygon/CanvasGeometry/CanvasBatch/GPUCanvasBackend/CompatibilityCanvasBackend and CanvasVertexProgram now carry [native point/line primitives](components/canvas-rendering.md#native-point-and-line-primitives). No production type or public declaration is added.

AnimatableBody synchronization now shares the current-group pose-authoring boundary, with immediate pending-target presentation and joined owner numerical preparation. [Contract and verification](components/physics-bodies.md#kinematic-synchronization-from-processing-groups). No production type or public declaration was added.

[Completed canvas counters](components/canvas-counters.md) use [ViewportRenderInfo](classes/ViewportRenderInfo.md), [ViewportRenderInfoType](classes/ViewportRenderInfoType.md) and [RenderingServer.RenderingInfo](classes/RenderingServer.RenderingInfo.md) for actual submitted geometry. Existing CanvasBatch/CanvasBackend/Viewport/RenderingServer/Performance retain the counter state and completion boundary; no independent renderer or profiling service is added.

[MissingNode](classes/MissingNode.md) now provides loader-created recovery of unavailable compiled node factories and linked scene files. Its bounded typed fields and retained connection records integrate with current archive, scene and duplication lifetimes. The internal UnresolvedSceneConnectionSchema metadata role and Node.RecordedConnectionLease are documented in [scene recovery internals](classes/UnresolvedSceneConnectionSchema.md).

[Compiled project batch execution](components/project-batch.md) now joins project operations, detached authoring, headless stepping and actual native capture through public runtime APIs. The tooling SDK types [BatchHost](classes/BatchHost.md), [BatchScenario](classes/BatchScenario.md), [BatchContext](classes/BatchContext.md) and [BatchMode](classes/BatchMode.md) remain in a separate development-tool assembly; the runtime adds six typed host-input overloads without changing existing signatures.

[HDR screen debanding](components/hdr-canvas.md#screen-debanding) now connects stored viewport/root-project controls and renderer-session overrides to final GPU dithering before byte output. Linear/offscreen images and movie capture retain their precision; no production type is added.

AndroidGameActivity/AndroidWindowHost now produce actual framework system back requests through DisplayServer.SystemBack.cs. The existing Node, Window and SceneTree types own notification 1007, GoBackRequested and QuitOnGoBack; no public platform adapter type is introduced. See [the connected runtime](components/window-runtime.md#system-back-navigation).

[Last-recovery one-way pair exclusions](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#last-recovery-one-way-pair-exclusions) preserve the final recovery decision through sweeps and contact projection across all selected implementations. Query-local value/device storage adds no production type or public signature.

[Final body-motion contact sampling](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#final-body-motion-contact-sampling) resamples margin-inclusive contact geometry after recovery and unsafe motion across CPU, resident GPU and the independent public provider. Completed departures clear obsolete contacts; no production type or public signature is added.

[Coincident rounded-core response](components/physics-contact-policy.md#coincident-rounded-cores) now shares CPU circle/capsule/segment primitive manifolds between actual dynamics and query/body-motion projection. The query-only fallback is removed; public scene/raw checks retain logical slots, physical invariants and zero warmed allocation across independently selected implementations.

CanvasItem TopLevel rebinding now connects to the existing recursive presentation reset, with allocation-free sampling propagation and [physical/pixel verification](components/canvas-rendering.md#toplevel-transition-resets-2026-10-18). This adds no production type or public declaration.

Last updated: 2026-10-21

[Failed initial collider preparation](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#authored-state-after-failed-initial-attachment) now discards partial numerical readback while retaining common body authoring. Ordinary retirement still captures accepted motion; no production type or public declaration is added.

[Initial collider attachment](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#initial-attachment-cleanup-errors) now preserves preparation and cleanup errors after retiring partial bindings for scene/raw Body/Area owners. Existing types and public signatures remain unchanged; all scene body siblings use the corrected PhysicsBody path.

[Space creation](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#space-creation-operation-audit) now has a complete public operation audit across explicit/named/lazy worlds, captured policies, readonly initialization queries, startup fallback and failure/resource/thread retirement. Existing PhysicsServer, PhysicsSpace and backend types retain their signatures and algorithms; no production type is added.

Private native libraries are owned by the Linux/macOS/Windows native packages in ordinary desktop builds; [native delivery](native-packaging.md) records source production and target verification limits. Windows resolution/full-suite checks are connected with execution pending. No public runtime type or managed backend assembly is added; internal floating-point atomics preserve Engine/audio snapshots on 32-bit hosts.

This is the exhaustive inventory of implemented Electron2D engine domains, components, and production types. Test-only helpers are not engine types.

Electron2D is 2D-only. Its game runtime targets Windows, macOS, Linux on X11 and Wayland, Android, iOS, Android TV, tvOS, and Web; its editor targets Windows, macOS, and Linux on X11 and Wayland under ADR 0021. Runtime type rows belong to `Electron2D.dll`. Approved managed SDL3-CS bindings are internal source in that assembly under ADR 0012; native libraries remain platform deployment files. The separate first-party editor and its internal scene builder are recorded under repository product boundaries, following ADR 0027.

| [Core](domains/core.md) | [Object lifecycle](components/object-lifecycle.md) | [`ElectronObject`](classes/ElectronObject.md) | [`ElectronObject.cs`](../src/Core/Object/ElectronObject.cs) | Current | Implemented and verified  Typed compiled indexed projections and object/component/collection read/write composition. Inherited atomic queued-deletion observation, readonly nonstored descriptor, and automatic pre-delete cancellation with retryable lifetime. Per-object ordinary signal policy and typed emission gateway in [ElectronObject.Events.cs](../src/Core/Object/ElectronObject.Events.cs). Compiled subclasses use ordinary typed C# enumerators with per-cursor state. The base constructor and transient internal ConstructionScope establish invocation-local factory provenance in [ElectronObject.Construction.cs](../src/Core/Object/ElectronObject.Construction.cs), including nested/Task/engine-worker creation and closed-context reference release. Public property-list metadata publication preserves caller-thread event and lifetime semantics. |

| [Physics](domains/physics.md) | [Backend selection and ownership](components/physics-backends.md) | [PhysicsWorldBackend](classes/PhysicsWorldBackend.md) | [PhysicsWorldBackend.cs](../src/Servers/Physics/PhysicsWorldBackend.cs) | Internal | Fresh per-world owner with selected initialization, transactional activation with committed-policy repair after partial publication, guarded flush/end-sync and empty-world intervals, complete interval/query/motion dispatch, policies/capacity/statistics and validated cached direct-state factories reaching real scene/raw callbacks before solver/worker borrowing; complete numerical/extension acceptance remains open. |
| [Physics](domains/physics.md) | [Backend selection and ownership](components/physics-backends.md) | [CPUPhysicsWorldBackend](classes/CPUPhysicsWorldBackend.md) | [CPUPhysicsWorldBackend.cs](../src/Servers/Physics/CPUPhysicsWorldBackend.cs) | Internal | Fresh per-world implementation, owned solver lifetime, complete built-in interval/direct-space dispatch, owned world policy, native capacity/statistics and selected full-step phases and engine-unit body-motion dispatch with retained scratch; public extension families remain open. Native collider handles are retained in the owner table and absent from common scene/server identities. Exact fixed-rotation disk/point CCD roots retain grazing impacts before normal native response. Destroyed CPU worlds release static-slot graph segments/color roots; fixed idle slot contexts are measured separately. Frame impulse records/index prepare two points per retained contact budget; observed native identity peaks grow the initial body-based capacity across active/dormant storage before the next interval. Contact-pair hashing prepares the matching half-full contact budget before Solve; [evolving-scene and storage checks](components/physics-scene-performance.md#cpu-contact-pair-capacity-2026-10-15) retain zero warmed allocation.; [adaptive geometric queries](components/physics-queries.md#adaptive-sweep-refinement)  Shared range joins preserve simultaneous owner/worker publication errors. |

Joined host contact decoding uses retained unbound workers without a CPU numerical world. |

| [Scene](domains/scene.md) | [Scene hierarchy](components/scene-hierarchy.md) | [`Node`](classes/Node.md) | [`Node.cs`](../src/Scene/Main/Node.cs), [`Node.Duplication.cs`](../src/Scene/Main/Node.Duplication.cs), [`Node.Replacement.cs`](../src/Scene/Main/Node.Replacement.cs), [`Node.PhysicsInterpolation.cs`](../src/Scene/Main/Node.PhysicsInterpolation.cs), [`Node.Diagnostics.cs`](../src/Scene/Main/Node.Diagnostics.cs), [`Node.Names.cs`](../src/Scene/Main/Node.Names.cs), [`Node.EditorState.cs`](../src/Scene/Main/Node.EditorState.cs) | Current | Stored descriptions, transient folding, selected edited-scene membership, target-owned editable flags and authoring events feed flattened typed scene capture. Independent runtime internal idle/physics switches and current-frame ordinary dispatch; Neutral tree/lifecycle base, subtree replacement, inherited physics-interpolation policy, owner-scoped unique paths and typed diagnostics  Resource endpoints and typed subtree operations; node-only queries ignore property subnames. Active-only cached absolute paths and exact span lookups cover lifecycle, typed errors and owner/current-group access. Owned name/type searches preserve internal order and case-sensitive Unicode wildcards. Readable/generated collision names, live separators, reserved-character normalization, unchanged/detached assignment delivery and retained reentrant path notifications preserve scene/physical identity. Debug-only weak orphan-node diagnostics preserve ID snapshots and logger-safe text without retaining hierarchies; Release has no node-ID scan or output; the separate weak Performance census works in both configurations. Entry/ready/exit stages retain typed hooks, failure cleanup and final detached-subtree signals. Borrowed authoring owners and reverse weak membership support detached unique-name scope and expiry; reparent completes possible phases after callback errors. All ten processing controls share coordinated reads/current-group writes; all six public callback switches have stored descriptors.  Cancellable queued deletion uses generation-qualified entries and prepared batch storage. Bound tween creation selects the attached tree or the explicit application SceneTree for detached nodes. Writable nonstored source-path metadata shares actual scene-origin and reload consumers. Automatic translation policy receives application language notifications through the active MainLoop. Runtime hierarchy duplication with typed flags, linked templates and retained source ownership. |

## Assembly inventory

| Assembly | Project | Target | State |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Electron2D.dll` | [`Electron2D.csproj`](../Electron2D.csproj) | `net10.0` and mobile SDK profiles | Managed-only `Electron2D` package and public engine assembly, including internal SDL3-CS source; explicit `Electron2D.{Platform}` packages deliver target-native assets |

This assembly row records the build and package contract. CharacterMovement is rendered and exercised on Linux x64/Wayland. The CI registry configures all eighteen target profiles, including separate Android, Apple and browser contract applications; production game hosting and native execution remain subject to the limits in the platform report.

## Repository product boundaries

| Product layer | Source root | Dependency direction | Current state |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Runtime engine | [`src/`](../src/) and [`Electron2D.csproj`](../Electron2D.csproj) | May use only approved runtime dependencies | Implemented types compile into `Electron2D.dll` |
| Self-hosted editor | [`editor/Electron2D.Editor.csproj`](../editor/Electron2D.Editor.csproj) | Executable references `Electron2D.dll`; runtime never references editor | Branded 1152×800 startup window with a compact shaded character and live font-rendered labels; internal [EditorScene](classes/EditorScene.md), public runtime host, desktop name/mark metadata and apphost VS Code F5 configuration; authoring remains absent |
| First-party games/examples | [`examples/`](../examples/) | Each executable references `Electron2D.dll`; runtime never references examples | [WaterPlayground](../examples/WaterPlayground/README.md): one resizable water playground, [WaterWindow](classes/WaterWindow.md) and [WaterSimulation](classes/WaterSimulation.md) and [WaterSurface](classes/WaterSurface.md), using public local compute, CPU density constraints and server rigid toys, with uniform bottom-anchored framing of a fixed world; rendered [first scene](../examples/CharacterMovement/README.md) with a resize-aware grid, live labels and an arrow-key-controlled character; [CharacterMovementScene](classes/CharacterMovementScene.md) consumes only the public runtime API |

Pinned NVorbis/NLayer sources now execute mono/stereo Ogg/MP3 inside Electron2D.dll; multichannel Vorbis retains the tested decoder prerequisite. Box2D.NET is already integrated internally in the physics component; FAudio and qoa-fu are integrated internally in audio. Backend/vendor helper types are implementation details and never exported.

Formatted texel buffers retain encoded bytes across native indexed reads, compute stores/atomics, ordinary transfers and graphics-to-canvas consumers. Versioned shader containers and one-time placeholder initialization retain the common validated compute/graphics/specialization path. |

| [Rendering](domains/rendering.md) | [Local device](components/local-compute.md) | [RDShaderFile](classes/RDShaderFile.md) | [RDShaderFile.cs](../src/Servers/Rendering/Device/RDShaderFile.cs) | Current | Named borrowed stage resources, copied scalar-sorted names, import diagnostics and portable aliased version graphs. |
| [Rendering](domains/rendering.md) | [Local compute](components/local-compute.md) | [RDTextureFormat](classes/RDTextureFormat.md), [RDTextureView](classes/RDTextureView.md), [RDSamplerState](classes/RDSamplerState.md) | [Device texture configuration](../src/Servers/Rendering/Device/RDTextureFormat.cs) | Copied 2D image/layer/mip descriptions and executing sampler configuration; native view, attachment and extended sampler dependencies remain explicit |
| [Rendering](domains/rendering.md) | [Local compute](components/local-compute.md) | [DataFormat](classes/RenderingDevice.DataFormat.md), [TextureType](classes/RenderingDevice.TextureType.md), [TextureUsageBits](classes/RenderingDevice.TextureUsageBits.md), [TextureSwizzle](classes/RenderingDevice.TextureSwizzle.md), [SamplerFilter](classes/RenderingDevice.SamplerFilter.md), [SamplerRepeatMode](classes/RenderingDevice.SamplerRepeatMode.md) | [Texture enums](../src/Servers/Rendering/Device/RenderingDevice.TextureEnums.cs) | Encoded format query domain, image/array roles, allocation/transfer usage and sampled view/filter/addressing selectors |

Proxies reuse the existing ServerTexture/RenderingTextureRegistry types and resolve root storage during replay; no additional public resource type or native backend is introduced.

SDL_image uses the internal Image binding module from SDL3-CS and selected platform assets. Web supplies a pinned SDL_image/libwebp static closure sharing its SDL core. PNG/JPEG/WebP/BMP/TGA/SVG file/buffer decoding and PNG/JPEG/WebP saving execute in native Linux and browser contract checks; Linux decoded PNG pixels also feed GPU textures. Pointer-sized SDL IO counts are checked through actual streams. Further codec APIs, browser presentation and full semantics remain separate.

## Explicitly absent

This inventory maps implemented production types. Missing mechanisms, exclusions and their exact prerequisites belong in [the coverage register](coverage/index.md) and the owning component/domain pages, rather than a second compatibility summary here. Runtime diagnostics and movie writing now have concrete production types listed in this inventory; their remaining backend and platform gates stay in their owning documents.

## Resizable split panels

| Domain | Component | Types | Source | Current implementation |

Private polyphonic Voice entries own bounded pending/active/retired state and prepared scratch, documented on the playback page.

The existing AudioServer/FAudioContext also own live output selection and cached driver chunk/queue duration through [AudioServer.Output.cs](../src/Servers/Audio/AudioServer.Output.cs), [FAudioContext.Output.cs](../src/Servers/Audio/FAudioContext.Output.cs) and [the native bridge](../tools/audio-native/output.c). No new public backend type is introduced.

Exact fixed-rotation disk/point CCD roots retain grazing impacts before normal native response. Destroyed CPU worlds release static-slot graph segments/color roots; fixed idle slot contexts are measured separately. Contact-pair hashing prepares the matching half-full contact budget before Solve; [evolving-scene and storage checks](components/physics-scene-performance.md#cpu-contact-pair-capacity-2026-10-15) retain zero warmed allocation. |
| [Physics](domains/physics.md) | [Selected body integration](components/physics-backends.md#selected-body-integration-preparation) | [PhysicsBodyRuntime](classes/PhysicsBodyRuntime.md), [CPUPhysicsColliderImplementation](classes/CPUPhysicsColliderImplementation.md), [GPUPhysicsColliderImplementation](classes/GPUPhysicsColliderImplementation.md) | [CPU preparation](../src/Servers/Physics/CPUPhysicsColliderImplementation.Integration.cs), [GPU preparation](../src/Servers/Physics/GPUPhysicsColliderImplementation.Integration.cs) | Internal | Common activity/reporting gates with selected pending/default-force preparation; CPU field validation and resident GPU policy retain actual numerical behavior. |

Selected field publication is in [PhysicsBodyRuntime.Fields](../src/Servers/Physics/PhysicsBodyRuntime.Fields.cs); resident exception and batch ownership are in [GPU exception policy](../src/Servers/Physics/GPUPhysicsWorldBackend.Exceptions.cs) and [portable observation](../src/Servers/Physics/GPUPhysicsWorldBackend.Portable.cs). These are implementation parts of the existing runtime/world types.

Selected body contact publication belongs to existing [PhysicsDirectBodyState/Extension](components/physics-body-extensions.md#selected-contact-publication), [PhysicsBodyContact](classes/PhysicsBodyContact.md), [RigidBody](classes/RigidBody.md) and PhysicsSpace. It retains native/projected buffers, owner-qualified immutable result construction and complete snapshots before monitors/callbacks; no new production type is introduced.

[RenderHandle](classes/RenderHandle.md) now supports owner-serialized replacement for exclusively owned private physics capacity buffers. [GPUPhysicsBodyStore](classes/GPUPhysicsBodyStore.md) retains those wrappers through native growth; no new production type or public native-handle API is added.

| [Physics](domains/physics.md) | [Value-only integration](components/physics-backends.md#value-only-body-integration-inputs) | [PhysicsBodyForceConfiguration](classes/PhysicsBodyForceConfiguration.md), [PhysicsBodyIntegrationConfiguration](classes/PhysicsBodyIntegrationConfiguration.md) | [Force input](../src/Servers/Physics/PhysicsBodyForceConfiguration.cs), [policy input](../src/Servers/Physics/PhysicsBodyIntegrationConfiguration.cs) | Internal | Actual scene/raw default-force intent and complete CPU/GPU authored integration values; no runtime/scene/native references cross these numerical input boundaries. |

| [Physics](domains/physics.md) | [Registered numerical solver adapter](components/physics-registered-adapter.md) | [PhysicsServerBackend](classes/PhysicsServerBackend.md), [PhysicsBackendCollider](classes/PhysicsBackendCollider.md), [PhysicsBackendJoint](classes/PhysicsBackendJoint.md), [PhysicsBackendOneWay](classes/PhysicsBackendOneWay.md), [PhysicsBackendShapeContact](classes/PhysicsBackendShapeContact.md), [PhysicsBackendOverlap](classes/PhysicsBackendOverlap.md), [PhysicsBackendStatistics](classes/PhysicsBackendStatistics.md), [RegisteredPhysicsWorldBackend](classes/RegisteredPhysicsWorldBackend.md) | [contract](../src/Servers/Physics/PhysicsServerBackend.cs), [binding](../src/Servers/Physics/RegisteredPhysicsWorldBackend.cs), [motion publication](../src/Servers/Physics/PhysicsColliderBackend.State.cs) | Public/internal | [Moving one-way continuation](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#moving-one-way-continuation) retains current approach and restored source-step timing through public scene/raw queries. [Complete selected pair operations](components/physics-shape-pairs.md#complete-selected-pair-operations) verify 64 ordered built-in combinations, logical pose/slot ownership and independent motion/cap policies. [Complete direct-space view acquisition](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#complete-direct-space-view-operation) verifies selected cache/factory/lifetime and six-query logical-origin/ordering/cap behavior. [Complete world parameter operations](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#complete-world-parameter-operations) exercise all nine typed settings through physical response and publication/history guards; the independent public-only consumer now retains value contact-cache history. Failure-only PhysicsColliderBackend.MotionPublication retains requested write arguments and is released after preparation, retirement or local rewind. Full logical identity/value dispatch into independently owned simulation; private Collider/Joint/History adapters and query scratch retain common identity/observer/lifecycle guarantees. Public registration and bounded custom geometry execute. [Initial retained object/canvas identity and priority](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#initial-collider-metadata) reach attachment hooks before shapes; live edits and world transfer preserve them. Portable episode capture validates current capacity, identity/shape/piece data and unordered uniqueness before encoding; the external consumer verifies real continuation. Scoped retained exception input reads and guarded body/joint policy edits execute with shared response/lifetime checks and zero warmed frame allocation. Unexercised operation acceptance remains open. Complete typed joint profile, connection guards and five-kind resource retirement/error continuation are exercised; shared shape preparation and scene reentry recover after failures. Bounded registered pin/groove/spring response and CPU/registered portable frame/replay checks execute; wider numerical acceptance remains open.; scoped motion-test RID/object exclusion membership is shared and verified. Shared world-scoped notification status is exposed to implementation and static space consumers; public selected shape/body pairs and immutable authored geometry access; validated motion-result receiver identity.; public-only explicit/scene lifecycle and completed-count verification, including bounded island output validation and allocation-free cached readers; selected collider publication/retirement failure is terminal across scene/server bodies and Areas, while notification errors remain recoverable  [Long contour pairs](components/physics-queries.md#long-pairs-of-flat-and-rounded-contours) verify continuous flat-core intervals and preserved geometric capsule contacts. |

| [Physics](domains/physics.md) | [Custom geometry](components/physics-custom-geometry.md) | [CustomShape](classes/CustomShape.md), [ShapeCollisionAlgorithm](classes/ShapeCollisionAlgorithm.md) | [resource](../src/Scene/Resources/CustomShape.cs), [data and pair algorithm](../src/Scene/Resources/ShapeGeometry.Custom.cs) | Public | Immutable custom format data, exact mass/bounds, diagnostic drawing, standalone pair dispatch, selected numerical participation and authoring-qualified replay. |

GPU body publication scheduling and its existing store/space roles are described in [changed publication](components/gpu-body-publication.md); adaptive transfer and valid-cache reuse add no public types or CPU solver mirror.

Registered body/Area shape and filter operations retain shared authoring and dispatch attached numerical hooks; initial/live borrowed writes reject before mutation. See [borrowed shape authoring](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#borrowed-shape-authoring). This adds no production declarations.

[Borrowed body authoring](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#borrowed-body-authoring) now guards scene/raw force, mass, state, integration and callback writes during numerical hooks, including direct-body views. Authored reads, ordinary callbacks and body-state forwarding retain access. The independent consumer exercises real impulses and zero warmed edited full-frame allocation; complete numerical/extension acceptance remains open.

[Registered Area fields](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#registered-area-fields) now exercise the complete typed profile/default shortcut through the selected hooks, including independent override/point/mask response and zero warmed edited full frames. Numerical-hook writes reject before mutation; authored reads and ordinary Area callback edits retain their guards and overlap history. Generic solver/extension acceptance remains open.

[Parameter reads](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#parameter-reads-and-preparation) project configured values without numerical preparation. Automatic inertia/center reads still synchronize current geometry outside numerical borrowing; preparation rejects inside a numerical hook. Initial/live scene/raw and zero-allocation public-only checks execute; full solver/extension acceptance remains open.

[Exact scene motion roles](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#exact-scene-motion-roles) now deliver RigidLinear for initial/live rotation lock, preserving freeze and disable precedence. The public-only consumer checks motion, guards, checkpoints and warmed full frames. Scene RID BodySetMode/BodyGetMode remain an explicit Partial family pending complete server-mode integration.

[Common server modes](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#common-server-mode-family) now project all four modes through scene and raw RIDs, with independent scene descriptors, effective state/force response, pose publication, reentry and replay. CPU/GPU/registered checks share the public consumer; complete solver and final performance acceptance remain open.

Integer device image consumers now validate float/signed/unsigned sampled scalar types and array roles, migrate reload bindings and use nearest native integer sampling. Cold snapshots expose raw-valued float32 RGBA; byte device readback preserves all integer bits. See [the integer canvas checkpoint](components/local-compute.md#integer-canvas-sampling-checkpoint) for executable evidence and the connected scalar-aware lighting and alpha-mask behavior.

| [Rendering](domains/rendering.md) | [Local device graphics](components/local-compute.md#color-graphics-and-compute-integration) | [RDVertexAttribute](classes/RDVertexAttribute.md), [RDAttachmentFormat](classes/RDAttachmentFormat.md), [RDPipelineRasterizationState](classes/RDPipelineRasterizationState.md), [RDPipelineDepthStencilState](classes/RDPipelineDepthStencilState.md), [RDPipelineMultisampleState](classes/RDPipelineMultisampleState.md), [RDFramebufferPass](classes/RDFramebufferPass.md), [RDPipelineColorBlendState](classes/RDPipelineColorBlendState.md), [RDPipelineColorBlendStateAttachment](classes/RDPipelineColorBlendStateAttachment.md) | [Graphics resources](../src/Servers/Rendering/Device/RenderingDevice.GraphicsPipeline.cs) | Copied vertex/attachment/raster/depth/stencil/blend descriptions and native color/depth/stencil pipelines with exact sample counts, color resolve and ordered input/preservation passes; borrowed vertex/index arrays and framebuffer textures; ordered layered multiview with shader view indices and per-layer input/depth/resolve |

RenderingDevice's [binary push recording](components/local-compute.md#binary-push-parameters) connects compute and paired graphics stages to copied original-layout parameters, without exposing native handles or private descriptors. The compiler adapter lives in [RenderingDevice.PushConstantSPIRV.cs](../src/Servers/Rendering/Device/RenderingDevice.PushConstantSPIRV.cs); list snapshots and owner validation live in [RenderingDevice.PushConstants.cs](../src/Servers/Rendering/Device/RenderingDevice.PushConstants.cs).


Area pose and monitor conformance belongs to existing [Area/PhysicsAreaRuntime/PhysicsSpace](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#area-pose-and-monitor-publication). Scene and server observer dispatch share the own-receiver configuration guard; no new production type is introduced.


Contact reporting depth belongs to existing [PhysicsBodyRuntime/PhysicsDirectBodyState/PhysicsBodyContact](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#contact-reporting-depth). Common policy filters selected signed scene-unit records and preserves immutable projection/history configuration; no new production type is introduced.

The existing [PhysicsNetwork public consumer](components/physics-network-example.md#stronger-process-acceptance-2026-10-12) reports verified GPU denial, full authoritative body/joint-author poses and angular motion. Engine public declarations and wire formats are unchanged; no production type is introduced.


Registered contact diagnostics dispatch capacity preparation through the selected PhysicsWorldBackend to PhysicsServerBackend.SetDebugContacts before solving, and validated bounded publication through ReadDebugContacts after synchronization. CPU retains its point cap; GPU prepares resident diagnostic storage. See [the diagnostic contract](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#diagnostic-capacity-and-publication).


Registered body/query factory hooks now execute through the public-only PhysicsBackendExtension.Consumer. Common view lifetime guards reject same-world numerical status/factory disposal across default, cached and independent views; ordinary and expired-context cleanup remain valid. PhysicsDirectBodyState.PrepareContacts seeds the cached empty snapshot, preserving independent caller-created dispatch and complete validated publication before callbacks. See [factory-selected direct views](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#factory-selected-direct-views).

Shape.CustomSolverBias now preflights all scene/raw borrowers before policy writes,
including equal assignments, disabled slots and borrowed server geometry views.
[The public registered/CPU/GPU consumer](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#borrowed-shape-correction-policy)
verifies owner/step/numerical/construction/reentry guards, ordinary callback edits,
observer-failure recovery, geometry/RID identity and 128 active warm frames at zero
owner/all-thread managed allocation. This shared resource policy guard does not
close arbitrary numerical-extension or final acceptance gates.

[Geometric authoring preflight](components/physics-registered-adapter.md) now protects all built-in setters,
CustomShape and procedural authors before private data changes. Registered/CPU/GPU
checks cover 16 paths, immutable data/RID preservation, ordinary callback/query
edits and warmed full frames. Arbitrary private subclass writes require the
protected authoring protocol; full numerical-extension acceptance remains open.

[Shared shape factories and data lifetime](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#shared-shape-construction-and-data-lifetime) now execute through the
public-only registered/CPU/GPU consumer. Replacement errors preserve original
geometry, continue every rollback/retirement and retain all failures; incomplete
borrowers retry during guarded preparation. Ten formerly unmapped extension rows
project to the common resource role as Partial. Full numerical-extension and final
physical/network/performance acceptance remain open.

[Body construction and continuous policy](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#body-construction-and-continuous-policy)
now share selected CPU/GPU/registered ownership, detached/live modes, automatic
mass reset and guarded typed configuration. Valid CCD writes avoid enum metadata
allocation after collection; the public-only consumer checks 128 edited full
steps at 0/0 managed bytes. Registered numerical CCD and full acceptance remain open.

[Registered pointer delivery](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#registered-pointer-delivery)
now verifies scene Body/Area policy through selected point queries, numerical
guards and 128 changed-policy full frames at zero managed allocation. Native
CPU/GPU/registered worlds each pass with both renderers and target pixel checks;
full registered geometry and final acceptance remain open.

The [rendered PhysicsNetwork client](components/physics-network-example.md#rendered-client-window)
now draws remote interpolation and local correction smoothing in a real window
while CPU authority/late client stay headless. Public snapshot and pixel checks
verify visible separation without physical-state mutation; renderer choice and
window FPS/allocation reporting remain separate from physics/replay metrics.

[Shape-owner metadata access](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#shape-owner-metadata-access)
now checks actual space ownership/failure for tree-less scene attachments while
retaining numerical authored reads and cleanup. The shared CPU/GPU/registered
consumer exercises every collider role and 128 full scalar-read frames at 0/0
managed bytes. Complete geometry/disable and final acceptance remain open.

Native font policies extend FontFile/SystemFont, FontData/FontVariation and authored/generated FontCachePage ownership. Versioned cache decoding, real native strike/autohinter pixels and MIP publication are tracked by [the text component](components/text.md#native-raster-policy-verification).

The existing ProjectSettings/SceneTree and Window/Control families now execute root and forced layout-direction project policies through the shared internal resolver; no additional public type is introduced. See [localization](domains/localization.md) for startup, domain, reflow and verification limits.

[Shared motion/query conformance](components/physics-queries.md) now executes the complete PhysicsMotionTests/PhysicsQueryTests/PhysicsShapeQueryTests on explicit public CPU/GPU worlds with preserved result/lifecycle/numerical assertions and owner/all-thread zero-byte warm gates. This records exercised query/result behavior, not full inherited/custom numerical or platform/performance acceptance.

[Shared body state/force/contact conformance](components/physics-bodies.md) now runs the complete state and thirteen-operation force suites on explicit CPU/GPU worlds, with actual momentum, deepest-cap scene/raw reporting, callback and view-lifetime assertions and strict owner/all-thread managed allocation gates. CPU native/private scheduling checks remain diagnostics; this does not close full inherited/custom numerical or platform/performance acceptance.

PhysicsServerStateTests now runs its complete typed state family on explicitly selected public CPU/GPU worlds: scene/raw/direct-view state, detached sleep and wake rules, axis projection, kinematic latest-target consumption, zero/inactive steps, detach/reentry, moving-support wakeup and scene-role presentation. PhysicsSurfaceVelocityTests also runs in the full CPU/GPU suite and the independent GPU-kinematic profile, retaining stationary normal/tangent/angular response, contact point velocity, character carry and additive target motion. Numerical bounds are unchanged. Both suites require 0/0 owner/all-thread managed bytes over 64 warmed active frames. Private native lock injection and historical staged controls remain separate CPU diagnostics. Native/platform, broader inherited/extension, networking and final performance acceptance remain open.

Window content rasterization introduces namespace-level ContentScaleMode, ContentScaleAspect and ContentScaleStretch enums and five stored Window properties. Viewport transforms/text raster scale, embedded windows and both canvas backends share the executable policy; see [Window](classes/Window.md#content-scale).

The complete OneWayCollisionTests, CollisionPolygonTests and PhysicsCollisionExceptionTests now execute on explicitly selected public CPU/GPU worlds. They retain opposite approaches, local/parent rotations, live side changes and zero direction, dynamic/kinematic platforms, Area sensor bypass, solid/hollow/concave polygon geometry, warning-error recovery and restored scene geometry. Unilateral scene/raw exceptions suppress both motion directions and solved response; duplicate entries, server-only/null and freed-target slots, live edits and reentry keep their existing rules. Position/velocity bounds are unchanged. Sixty-four warmed active one-way, polygon and exception frames plus 64 changed raw-exception/step cycles require 0/0 owner/all-thread managed bytes. Internal GPU store probes remain diagnostics; broader inherited/extension, network/platform, native allocations and final performance acceptance remain open.

The ordinary full suite now executes both CPU and independent GPU variants of all sixteen previously parameterized default-CPU families: AnimatableBody, CapsuleShape, CharacterBody, CollisionDisableMode, ConcavePolygonShape, ConvexPolygonShape, DampedSpringJoint, GrooveJoint, PhysicsBodyParameter, PhysicsServerJoint, PinJoint, RigidFreezeMode, SegmentShape, SeparationRayShape (including its dynamics suite), ShapeOwner and World. ELECTRON2D_TEST_SHARED_PHYSICS=cpu|gpu runs the complete same set explicitly; existing numerical and lifecycle/error assertions are unchanged. WorldTests retains the default-CPU/mixed-world cases and adds two explicitly selected isolated worlds, query identity/isolation, attachment-view invalidation and a shared once-per-tick step. Its 96 warmup/64 measured complete frames with lookups require 0/0 owner/all-thread bytes; one-frame 60-unit/s travel stays within .02 scene units, covering default damping and floating-point integration. Individual existing suite allocation scopes remain their documented limits. This closes the full-suite call-site omission, not remaining inherited/extension, native/platform, network and final performance acceptance.

Animation resources now execute built-in float/double/Vector2 key reduction through `Animation.Optimize`; existing library change observers invalidate player/mixer/graph bindings. The [key optimization contract](components/scene-animation.md#key-optimization) records preserved curve families, tolerances and verification limits.

The complete PhysicalBoneTests now executes on explicitly selected public CPU/GPU worlds, including command/follow and custom integration fixtures and restored graphs in a separate fresh process. It retains motion/filter identity, strength/disable/follow/freeze behavior, binding/hierarchy recovery, actual pin constraints, named commands, modifier failure rollback, phase/owner guards and existing numerical bounds. The ordinary full suite runs both backends; ELECTRON2D_TEST_PHYSICAL_BONE=1 with ELECTRON2D_PHYSICAL_BONE_BACKEND=cpu|gpu selects one. After 128 warmup frames, 128 complete physics/pose/weighted Polygon cycles require 0/0 owner/all-thread managed bytes. Native hosts select physics independently from ELECTRON2D_PHYSICAL_BONE_RENDERER=gpu|compatibility, reject renderer fallback and assert both actual selections. All four CPU/GPU-physics by hardware-renderer combinations verify real changed skin pixels and matching Bone/body pose; 64 prepared draw/velocity-update intervals require zero owner-thread managed bytes. This rendering interval is separate from full physical allocation measurement. Native allocations, large rigs, foreign platforms, human acceptance and final network/massive GPU/window FPS gates remain open.

Animation and AnimationLibrary now have exact resource-file factories. ResourceFileTypes.RegisterAnimationProperty reconstructs typed property/Bézier tracks from stable compiled bindings; timing, metadata, keys, resource/library aliases and colored markers persist through the existing graph archive. Method/audio/nested and exact mixer/player node schemas now execute; graph definitions now have exact file schemas; RESET save preparation now executes through PackedScene.PackForSave.

The complete PhysicsBodyTests, RigidBodyForceTests, RigidBodyContactTests and ShapePairEventTests now execute on explicitly selected public CPU/GPU worlds, with actual backend assertions in every numerical scene. The ordinary full suite runs both variants; ELECTRON2D_TEST_SCENE_PHYSICS=cpu|gpu selects the same complete set. Existing force/impulse/center units and numerical bounds, freeze/lock/mass/geometry recovery, persistent force packing, sleep/wake notification policy, contact caps/deduplication and object-before-shape entry/departure ordering are unchanged. Scene/raw nullable payloads, compound logical slots, handler mutation/errors and committed-event no-replay remain asserted. All existing 64-frame warm gates now require 0/0 owner/all-thread managed bytes for resting/active/moving bodies, forces, monitored/no-contact frames and steady/transitioning shape pairs. Box2D SIMD and worker scheduling probes remain CPU diagnostics; they do not establish independent GPU acceptance. Native allocations, wider inherited/extension, foreign platforms, full networking and final GPU/window performance acceptance remain open.
Animation method, audio and nested-player tracks now have portable typed schemas. ResourceFileTypes.RegisterAnimationMethod reconstructs compiled callbacks with exact receiver/argument roles; AudioStreamWAV has an exact encoded-payload factory. Shared resource keys/streams retain graph aliases; native/queued state stays transient; exact mixer/player scene factories now reconstruct authored configuration, and graph definitions now reconstruct through exact typed schemas.

The public-only custom annulus/circle consumer now verifies independent swept-region truth and true outer-capsule/inner-lens boundary contacts through standalone and selected resource pairs, including reversal and warmed zero managed allocation. The production custom geometry contract is unchanged; arbitrary numerical-extension acceptance remains open.

AnimationMixer/Player scene storage now restores authored namespaces, shared libraries, callback/mixing/playback options and next/blend policy through exact factories. AnimationControllerPersistenceTests exercises fresh processes and actual loaded-scene pixels; graph and RESET save preparation now executes.

PackedScene.PackForSave executes detached RESET property/Bézier preparation with deep resource isolation and ownership transfer; inherited AnimationMixer.ResetOnSave stores the policy across Mixer/Player/Tree. Runtime Pack retains the actual-pose capture contract.

The existing Engine and RenderingServer types now own the [explicit native frame contract](components/manual-render-frames.md): manual draw, GPU synchronization, ordered one-shot callbacks and process-lifetime completed-frame accounting. No new production type is added.

The existing CPUPhysicsWorldBackend fixed joint endpoint now prepares solver/contact-motion storage for its added body slot; [retained graph measurement and capacity regression](components/physics-retained-memory.md) add development tools without new production types or signatures.

The existing CPUPhysicsWorldBackend cast/CCD helper, internal B2Distances geometric query policy and resident GPU query/motion shaders now preserve geometric first touch; [the compound checks](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#compound-slot-numerics) record scope and limits. No new production type or public signature is added.

`SceneConnectionBinding<TPublisher, TSubscriber, TBindings>` implements compiled authored event connections with typed payloads, dual endpoint cleanup and PackedScene file reconstruction (ADR 0010).

Viewport.UpdateMouseCursorState and Input default-cursor changes execute internal stationary-pointer motion through existing GUI routing, with safe-point coalescing and passive physics hover.

RenderingServer.CanvasItemSetVisibilityNotifier supplies typed retained-canvas enter/exit callbacks with multi-viewport sampling, cull/clip bounds and deterministic runtime callback ownership.

ResourceLoader.ListDirectory discovers recognized resource leaf names and immediate directory names for current loaders and native formats, with fresh-process discovery/load verification.

Control pointer capture transfer and local-to-native warp execute through SceneTree viewport GUI state and existing coordinate transforms; deferred callback targets remain transient.

OS restart-on-exit captures a validated copied argument list for normal desktop process shutdown and launches through the existing current-application process path. Owned/borrowed loop cleanup and native rendering remain ordinary Engine responsibilities.

[ShaderInclude](classes/ShaderInclude.md) is an implemented Resource with stored Code, source/archive persistence and executable HLSL/GLSL import consumption.

TranslationServer now supplies the fixed locale catalog, aliases/script defaults and gettext rules; Translation resources automatically select integer plural forms with preserved caller overrides, copy/archive state and native localized-consumer verification.

Translation catalog import uses internal TranslationCatalogLoader and CatalogPluralRule within the runtime assembly. Public Translation.PluralRulesOverride preserves imported definitions; no separate public parser or loader type is introduced.

RenderingServer.CanvasSetItemRepeat reuses RenderingCanvasItemState and retained canvas composition; no new public type is introduced.

RenderingServer.CanvasGroupMode is the four-value retained compositor selector. CanvasItemSetCanvasGroupMode reuses internal CanvasGroupSettings and the existing scene-group/mask kernels; no public state wrapper is introduced.

order, stop before nested Window scopes and continue after callback failures.
WindowNotificationTests exercises eight event kinds through embedded/public and SDL
dummy routes plus 1024 zero-byte warmed operations. DPI/position platform limits and
missing go-back/HDR sources remain open; see [window runtime](components/window-runtime.md#containing-window-notifications).

Point-field reduction avoids float squared-distance underflow/overflow and
retains signed constant/inverse-square behavior, exact-center zero and rejection
of truly unrepresentable forces. PhysicsPointGravityRangeTests covers scene, raw
Area and default-space sources with three body roles and CPU zero-allocation frames.
The revised device kernel compiles; real GPU numeric-range acceptance remains open.
See [point-field verification](components/physics-areas.md#point-gravity-numeric-range).


[Software Vulkan validation](components/physics-backends.md#software-vulkan-functional-checks) now executes
the resident GPU modules, point-range/failure checks and common public physics suites
without physical-GPU load. A separate CPU authority/GPU prediction/late CPU client
run covers the existing impaired-network scenario. Hardware GPU execution, performance
and full-goal acceptance remain open; software timing is not hardware evidence.

Retained canvas item presentation uses renderer-session physics histories shared with light/occluder transforms. Static interpolation selection, item-local reset and atomic coordinate rebasing execute on caller-owned and scene identities without storing native history in scenes. [Canvas interpolation](components/canvas-rendering.md#retained-canvas-item-interpolation) records native and managed checks.


Point-gravity center transformation and coordinate subtraction retain widened CPU
intermediates before normalization. The shared test covers opposite finite 2e38
coordinates, signed direction and falloff, affine virtual centers, three body roles
and zero-allocation full frames. Collision precision at such coordinates and real
GPU performance remain separate gates. See [field numeric range](components/physics-areas.md#point-gravity-numeric-range).

The continuous-contract entry now accepts separate numerical execution without
being redirected to the general CPU suite. Both CPU/GPU profiles pass the same
CCD, infinite-boundary and directed-ray cases on software Vulkan, with explicit
0/0 owner/all-thread warm allocation gates. See [current software conformance](components/cpu-continuous-collision.md#software-vulkan-continuous-conformance-2026-10-14).
Hardware performance, native allocation and foreign-platform acceptance remain open.

[Shared public contact correction](components/physics-contact-policy.md#shared-public-correction-2026-10-14)
now runs the complete shape/world bias, slack, resource/lifetime and scene sleep/event
suite on explicitly selected CPU/GPU worlds in serial/separate numerical modes.
Seven forms include both bias-one endpoints; 128 measured edited full steps require
0/0 owner/all-thread managed bytes. Software Vulkan execution and historical direct
resident/stage controls remain distinct from hardware/performance acceptance.

Retained canvas item copy settings now share the existing screen-snapshot compositor with BackBufferCopy, including source republishing and session-only storage. See [copy settings](components/canvas-rendering.md#retained-backbuffer-copy-settings).

[Shared public priority verification](components/physics-contact-policy.md#shared-public-priority-2026-10-14)
now exercises weighted recovery, scene/server/CharacterBody motion, packing and
lifetime through explicit CPU/GPU worlds in both numerical-thread modes. Changing
priority preserves sleep and fixture identity over 128 measured full frames at
0/0 owner/all-thread managed bytes; query brackets retain the same zero budget.
Software Vulkan execution does not establish hardware or broader acceptance.

[Shared public contact history](components/physics-contact-policy.md#shared-public-history-2026-10-14)
now checks seven geometries, live reuse limits, actual support/impulses and scene
sleep/event ordering through explicit CPU/GPU worlds in both numerical-thread modes.
All six public/resident/stage profiles retain 128 edited full-step samples at
0/0 owner/all-thread managed bytes. Software Vulkan evidence leaves hardware,
registered geometry and broader network/platform/performance acceptance open.

[Shared public iteration verification](components/physics-contact-policy.md#shared-public-iterations-2026-10-14)
now exercises captured/live counts, contact/pin convergence, momentum, fixed-time
integration and sleep/phase guards on explicit CPU/GPU worlds in both numerical
thread modes. All six profiles preserve 256 active bodies and 1/16/32-sweep full
step brackets with 0/0 owner/all-thread managed bytes. Hardware and broader
registered/network/platform/performance acceptance remain open.

[Shared public sleep verification](components/physics-sleep.md#shared-public-sleep-policy-2026-10-14)
now checks the complete strict threshold/duration, callback/lifetime and scene event
suite on explicit CPU/GPU worlds in both numerical-thread modes. All six profiles
retain 64 active or wake/sleep-cycling bodies at 0/0 warm owner/all-thread bytes.
The public-only CPU/GPU/registered consumer additionally verifies connected groups
and quiet-clock restoration. Hardware and broader acceptance remain open.

[Public angular CCD](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#angular-compound-motion)
now checks asymmetric mass-center motion and intermediate rotational impact on
CPU/GPU/registered worlds in serial/separate modes. Exact slots, opposite frame
impulses, authored CCD modes and one callback/tick retain their contract; both
128-warm/128-measured impact brackets require 0/0 managed bytes. GPU executes on
CPU-only llvmpipe; hardware, broader geometry and final acceptance remain open.

[Mass roles](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#mass-profiles-across-roles)
and [portable role correction](components/physics-snapshots.md#portable-body-roles)
now execute explicit CPU/GPU/registered serial and separate numerical profiles.
Scene/raw automatic/custom centers, inverse dynamics, point velocity and exact
slot queries survive role changes; CPU/GPU round trips preserve pending force/
torque consumption and atomic authoring rejection. Original warm brackets retain
0/0 managed bytes. GPU uses llvmpipe; hardware and broader acceptance stay open.

[Portable contact and motion history](components/physics-snapshots.md#portable-contact-and-motion-history)
now passes CPU/CPU, CPU/GPU and CPU/registered serial/separate public-only profiles.
Imported points/impulses and character slides retain destination peer/slot identity;
raw Area/contact history stays silent during correction and avoids repeated enters.
Malformed indices/platforms preserve state and events, and real movement retains
platform carry. Warm contact cycles require 0/0 bytes; cold motion validation has
no allocation claim. Hardware and wider geometry/joint/network gates remain open.

[Capture topology](components/physics-snapshots.md#capture-after-topology-edits)
and [same-count authoring validation](components/physics-snapshots.md#same-count-authoring-validation)
now pass CPU/CPU, CPU/GPU and CPU/registered serial/separate public-only profiles.
Each capture profile retains 24 removal/reindex/readdition cases; each geometry
profile retains 14 incompatible/restored cases across both directions. Rejection
preserves complete packets, sampled values and events; real refresh/continuation
recovers. These cold structural checks have no allocation claim. Hardware and
broader geometry/joint/network acceptance remain open.

[Portable joint continuation](components/physics-snapshots.md#portable-joint-continuation)
now passes CPU/CPU, CPU/GPU and CPU/registered serial/separate public-only profiles.
Scene/raw pin/groove/spring frames, atomic policy rejection, private replay and
continued constraints retain their bounds; all six warm cases require 0/0 bytes.
[Off-center spring checks](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#off-center-spring-anchors-and-portable-joints)
also retain force/moment/cap and stable damping bounds. GPU executes on llvmpipe;
hardware, broader geometry/graphs and network acceptance remain open.

[Object identity and class ancestry](classes/ElectronObject.md#m-electron2d-electronobject-isclass-system-string)
now execute through IsClass, immutable InstanceID/ClassName and the existing C#
Dispose/ToString projections. Shared CPU/GPU serial/separate scene tests retain
Body/Area/Resource branches, compiled subclasses, alias rejection and post-retirement
metadata, with 0/0 warm full-frame bytes. Other inherited APIs and final acceptance
remain separate gates.

ElectronObject and its typed PropertyDescriptor family retain exact teardown-thread inspection and strict disposal-time writes. [ObjectPropertyTests](../tests/Electron2D.Tests/ObjectPropertyTests.cs) verifies discovery/defaults/errors and selected CPU/GPU full-frame property flows; [the owning lifecycle component](components/object-lifecycle.md) records the verification limits.

[Extended event arities](classes/EventConnection.md) cover current 3-argument picking and 4-argument body/Area shape signatures under ADR 0010 without altering event payloads. The shared physics fixture now retains 128 measured emissions of each arity, 64 body and 64 shape enters/exits and exact RID/owner/disabled-first shape indices at strict 0/0 warm bytes on selected CPU/GPU serial/separate profiles. Deferred/one-shot calls retain their separate capture allocation paths.

EventConnection retains an immutable callback for reusable zero-argument deferred delivery; prepared scene queue/frame execution verifies zero per-emission managed allocation. [The connection component](components/event-connections.md) records argument capture, growth and acceptance limits.

The [Image block-codec slice](components/image-bcn.md) compiles pinned BCnEncoder.NET and buffer-tool sources as private types in Electron2D.dll. Vendored types are implementation dependencies, not production API inventory entries. Image.BCn.cs, Image.DDS.cs and Image.DDS.Pixels.cs own the public compression/DDS behavior and portable layout adapters.

The [point-field hardware guard](components/physics-areas.md#hardware-point-field-guards-2026-10-14) updates the existing PhysicsAreaFields/GPUPhysicsBodyStore reduction slice, preserving its public types and native status/publication boundary.

WebP lossless/lossy saving, live codec settings, compressed-source conversion, atomic files and texture/capture consumers are described in [the executable codec contract](components/image-webp.md). Foreign-target native execution and prebuilt bundle publication remain unverified.

The [EXR I/O slice](components/image-exr.md) supplies precision-preserving HDR file/buffer load and lossless save. Image and texture resource discovery includes DDS and EXR alongside PNG/JPEG/WebP/BMP/TGA/SVG; typed archives also expose their registered extensions. GPU float sampling, explicit Compatibility byte conversion and float-driver rejection are separate exercised profiles.

Theme archive/startup integration adds Theme.Persistence.cs and ThemeDB.Project.cs to the existing Theme/ThemeDB types, with typed project settings and shared Control/Window lookup. It introduces no runtime assembly or backend type.

The [registered rectangle consumer](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#registered-rectangle-numerics)
now verifies actual rectangle/circle regions, mass, contacts, queries and CCD behind
PhysicsServerExtension, retaining its public-only implementation and current
production type inventory. This adds no shipped backend type or runtime API.

The [registered capsule consumer](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#registered-capsule-numerics)
adds independent rounded-segment numerics behind the existing extension family.
This introduces no shipped backend type or runtime API.

The [registered segment consumer](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#registered-segment-numerics)
adds independent line/zero-area numerics behind the existing extension family;
no shipped backend type or runtime API is introduced.

The [registered convex consumer](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#registered-convex-numerics)
adds independent whole-contour numerics behind the existing extension family.
No shipped backend type or runtime public API is introduced.

FoldableContainer.cs, FoldableContainer.Layout.cs and FoldableContainer.Storage.cs implement the GUI foldable title/body consumer; FoldableGroup.cs supplies its scene-local selection resource. BarPosition.cs is shared by FoldableContainer and TabContainer. ThemeDB.Foldable.cs extends the retained default catalog; no new backend/runtime assembly is introduced.

[Registered infinite-boundary numerics](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#registered-boundary-numerics)
now exercise analytic planes in the independent public-only provider, all six
query families and reverse query source, Area/contacts, zero geometric mass,
material/surface impulses, private replay, sleep/lifecycle and translating/rotating
CCD. Twenty full-frame brackets per CPU/GPU/provider serial/separate profile
require strict 0/0 warmed managed bytes. CPU authored body/query/joint angles now
use ordinary sine/cosine; the far rotated query retains its .005-unit bound rather
than absorbing the former approximate-angle drift. Directed-ray, arbitrary/coupled,
complete inherited/extension/network/native/platform and hardware-performance

[Registered directed-ray numerics](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#registered-directed-numerics)
now execute surface entry, axis/slope separation, zero geometric mass, all query
families and reverse source, raw recovery and character snap, material/surface
impulses, silent replay and linear/angular CCD through an independent public-only
provider. Twenty-three full-frame brackets per CPU/GPU/provider serial/separate
profile require 128 warmup/128 measured frames at 0/0 managed bytes. Directional
response and stable long-ray/circle roots are corrected in the shared consumer.
Arbitrary custom/coupled, complete inherited/extension/network/native/platform
and hardware-performance acceptance remain open; coverage states are unchanged.


[Registered priority recovery](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#registered-priority-recovery)
now exercises weighted motion recovery independently through the public API,
including finite/directed families, bounded contact planes, exclusions, one-way
controls, character movement and unchanged rigid momentum. The shared CPU/GPU/
registered profile covers five 128-warmup/128-measured full-frame brackets at zero
managed allocation. Wider registered contact-cache settings and arbitrary coupled correction and complete numerical,
native/network/platform/performance acceptance remain open.

IP.API.cs supplies the public static delegates; Engine registers the retained identity. NetworkSockets and HTTPClient consume the same backend/cache. Internal controlled resolver fixtures remain within runtime/tests.

[Moving one-way recovery](components/gpu-resident-motion-queries.md#moving-one-way-recovery)
now includes kinematic/dynamic linear approach over the last physical step on
both production backends, including private/portable restoration. Static surface,
receding/tangent and angular-only velocities do not enlarge recovery depth.
PhysicsMovingOneWayTests checks raw/scene callers, roles, rotated slots, six finite
source families and eight 128-warmup/128-measured complete-frame brackets at zero
managed allocation. Native/platform/hardware/performance acceptance remains open.

## Shared node frame time (2026-10-14)

Node process/physics delta observations now read the attached tree's current or
most recent lane duration, including nodes without callbacks and disabled/paused
nodes. The tree commits time before frame-start observers; detachment returns zero
and reattachment adopts the receiving tree. Owner/lifetime checks apply. Manual
notifications do not advance time. CharacterBody.MoveAndSlide uses physics time
only during a physics frame and process time otherwise, with no cross-lane or
first-frame guess. Zero duration contributes no velocity travel; penetration
recovery remains executable.

PhysicsNodeDeltaTests uses controller-driven public character movement on selected
CPU/GPU ordinary/separate modes, node lifecycle/error observations and scaled/zero
Engine time. Two complete-frame brackets warm 128 and measure 128 at strict 0/0
owner/all-thread managed bytes. Existing CharacterBody, internal-lane/hierarchy,
surface, ray, snapshot and checkpoint checks retain their numerical assertions;
external callers explicitly prepare their process lane rather than relying on the
removed fallback. The initial CPU/GPU regression moved zero instead of three scene
units. Evidence is retained under bin/physics-node-delta/2026-10-14. Full inherited,
native, foreign and hardware/window/performance acceptance remains open.

## Automatic typed callbacks (2026-10-14)

Ready notification now enables each overridden process, physics, input, shortcut,
unhandled-key and unhandled-input callback before OnReady. Detached defaults are
false; OnReady and later setters can disable the lanes. Consumed ready does not
repeat on ordinary reentry; RequestReady re-arms it. Manual ready notification
runs the same preparation without consuming managed readiness. Inherited overrides
participate, while hidden non-virtual methods and plain nodes stay disabled.

The runtime inspects only six strongly bound virtual delegate targets on cold
first-type preparation and caches a bit mask under weak CLR Type keys. It retains
no node/delegate, performs no name lookup/assembly scan/reflection invocation and
adds no frame work. Ordinary typed dispatch, pause/membership/owner/error guards,
internal lanes and selected CPU/GPU numerical ownership stay in place.

PhysicsAutomaticCallbackTests uses real public character motion and all input
stages, detached/ready flags, inherited/individual/hidden overrides, live disabling,
reentry, requested/manual ready, packed reconstruction, pause and weak retirement.
A complete physics/idle/input bracket warms 128 and measures 128 at strict zero
owner/all-thread managed bytes. A public-only standalone consumer supports the
same compiled test in ordinary and NativeAOT hosts. Evidence and exact limits are
retained under bin/physics-auto-callbacks/2026-10-14; full inherited, native/foreign
and hardware/window/performance acceptance remains open.

[GPUPhysicsBodyStore](classes/GPUPhysicsBodyStore.md) packs selected contact rows under a frame-record/request-multiplicity bound with one completion and validated caller-segment scatter; its retained bookkeeping contains no numerical mirror. It also includes [internal buffer accounting](components/gpu-memory.md) for all requested storage/transfer resources, transient growth overlap and checkpoint retirement; no new production type or public API is introduced. Its [validated device color cache](components/gpu-contact-colors.md#validated-device-color-cache-2026-10-18) uses the same tracked buffer ownership, retains local GPU continuation state and leaves portable wire payloads unchanged.

Engine.Movie.cs also owns configured RunMovie/RunMovieAsync startup over the existing MovieWriter family; it adds no production type. File/FPS/disable-VSync project descriptors share the existing registry and native window lifecycle.

[Shape-edit recovery and warmed reuse](components/physics-queries.md#failed-raw-shape-edits-and-warmed-reuse) adds no production declarations. Raw transactions restore history/geometry after errors; CPU fixture/sensor scratch and GPU empty sensor-query wrappers retain only prepared capacity. One-way public records remain immutable with unchanged-value reuse and separately measured changed-policy publication. Complete numerical/native/driver/hardware acceptance remains open.

[Failed filter publication](components/physics-filters.md#failed-updates-and-effective-sensing) uses existing CollisionObject/PhysicsColliderBackend/PhysicsSpace owners: commit authoring after numerical success, retain two boolean pending markers and repair effective metadata before queries or an active interval. CPU Area scans use effective body layers, including inactive PhysicalBone. No production type or public declaration is added.

geometry and common resource state into concrete wrappers while borrowing all
using worlds; both owned import and borrowed-RID export support no-factory Shapes.

adds no production type or public declaration. PhysicsServer retains world borrows
through numerical rebuild/rollback/failed retirement and releases them before
committed old-view retirement. Existing collider/world owners remain authoritative.

also reject incoming RID/resource bindings from unrelated worlds and edits or
release of detached participants during data reads/replacements. Public-only
checks retain exact slot/property identity, error propagation and successful
retry/binding after the guard ends; full numerical/network/performance acceptance
remains open.

participating world through removal and private-view retirement. Status failure
preserves live data; committed cleanup attempts all users, retains exact errors
and unregisters the RID. Public-only checks retain unrelated slots, exact Area
entry/exit histories and zero warmed managed bytes. Wider acceptance stays open.

[Node group membership](components/node-groups.md) preserves first-add persistence, literal identifiers,
current processing-group access and exact physical/file consumers. Physical
mutations retain their owner guard before virtual consumer hooks. Runtime
Node duplication retains its separate ADR 0091 binding/identity prerequisite.

[Unsigned image texture sampling](components/integer-textures.md) extends existing TexturePixels/GPUTexture, ordinary/layered resources, imports and rendering identities without adding a public type.

Window's [placement family](classes/Window.md#initial-placement-and-minimum-size-reset) includes the nested WindowInitialPosition enum, its stored policy and constrained ResetSize, integrated with native root startup and embedded entry/show.

TranslationDomain and TranslationServer now share scored runtime catalog selection, weakly retained identity preparation and current FindTranslations queries. Physical inherited domain/plural consumers and actual Wayland regional-resource glyphs are verified in [localization](components/localization.md#scored-runtime-lookup).

TranslationDomain prepares Unicode pseudolocalization per live input identity and transform policy. Source code-point masking/expansion, placeholders, BIDI, cache retirement and physical/actual glyph consumers are verified in [localization](components/localization.md#unicode-pseudolocalization).

[Logical transform observation](components/scene-hierarchy.md#logical-transform-observation-from-processing-groups) now permits active groups to read local/global Entity and Control chains, including inherited physical nodes. Shared dirty matrices publish under the retained canvas-item gate after virtual getters return; notification coalescing, ordinary-worker rejection and current-group pose mutation guards remain distinct. Public parallel ordinary/pool-host physics consumers verify the bounded read contract. General worker mutation remains Partial.

[Transform authoring from processing groups](components/scene-hierarchy.md#transform-authoring-from-processing-groups) now covers all Entity pose setters/helpers and inherited physical/drawable nodes. Cache revisions and a short notification queue gate preserve committed pose/error semantics; existing scene spaces are borrowed for metadata edits and released after every joined stage before selected queries/stepping. The original scene owner temporarily uses current-group rights while helping a parallel stage. General inherited worker operations and native physical writes retain separate coverage.

[Transform notification policies and forcing](components/scene-hierarchy.md#transform-notification-policies-and-forcing-from-groups) now accept their processing-group contexts. Forced user notifications remain immediate; camera viewport and generated tile physics/navigation updates publish after the joined worker stage before owner queries/stepping. Shared queue cancellation and lifecycle accounting support concurrent forcing without holding gates across callbacks. Inherited interpolation reads permit active groups; mode mutation and reset permit the owner/current group. Reset callbacks remain immediate, with foreign branches reported and later siblings attempted. Ordered camera scroll/reset and borrowed renderer policies publish after the joined worker stage; see [interpolation publication](components/scene-hierarchy.md#physics-interpolation-from-processing-groups).

General notification traversal now runs immediately in the owner/current processing group, including physical descendants, and preserves foreign-branch errors, callback reentry and protected geometry ownership. Canvas identity and effective participation now use joined owner publication. Cast, configured skeletal and GUI consumers retain independent guards and open coverage. See [verification](components/scene-hierarchy.md#general-notification-traversal-from-processing-groups).

[Physical canvas notification publication](components/scene-hierarchy.md#physical-canvas-notifications-from-processing-groups) now preserves current-group body/Area/tile identity requests and effective participation through the joined owner phase. Logical canvas-layer reads accept active groups; attachment epochs and exact generated RID targets protect owner reentry. General reserved notification consumers remain Partial.

[Scene cast group access](components/physics-queries.md#scene-cast-metadata-and-cached-observations-from-groups) now covers local options, exceptions and held results. Numerical sampling remains owner-only, as in the pinned server; it is no longer misclassified as an absent reserved notification consumer. Other reserved consumers remain Partial.

The [one-way slot operation audit](components/physics-shapes.md#one-way-slot-operation-audit)
completes the existing PhysicsServer/PhysicsBackendCollider setter projection;
no production type or runtime behavior is added. Raw/manual/child failure/retry,
full policy reclaim, selected motion/recovery and strict warmed full frames are
verified separately from complete numerical-extension acceptance.

The [custom bias accessor audit](components/physics-contact-policy.md#custom-bias-accessor-audit)
completes the existing shared Shape.CustomSolverBias implementation getter/setter
projection. Numerical hook/thread ownership, resource lifecycle and actual selected
correction/impulse behavior are verified; no production type or runtime behavior
is added. Complete extension/native/platform/network/performance gates stay open.

The [body-pair publication correction](components/physics-shape-pairs.md#body-pair-publication-recovery-and-composed-pose-validation)
uses existing PhysicsSpace/PhysicsBodyRuntime scene/raw preparation before selected
BodyCollideShape dispatch. No production type is added; caller-output/epoch and
queued-kinematic behavior are verified separately from full numerical acceptance.

The [pickability operation audit](components/physics-picking.md#pickability-operation-audit)
completes two existing common Body/Area input operations through selected point
queries. No production type, public declaration or runtime behavior is added;
complete extension/numerical/network/platform/performance gates stay open.

The [filter setter audit](components/physics-filters.md#filter-setter-operation-audit)
completes four existing common Body/Area layer/mask operations through selected
UpdateFilter. No production type, API or runtime behavior is added. Complete
registered numerical/extension/network/platform/performance gates stay open.

The [Area field input correction](components/physics-areas.md#prepared-area-field-inputs)
reuses existing PhysicsServer/PhysicsSpace prepared input guards for ten configured
getters across scene/raw/default profiles. No production type is added. Public
thread/read/write/failure and real field-frame evidence remains separate from full
parameter/numerical/platform/performance acceptance.

[Body mode operation audit](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#body-mode-operation-audit) verifies the existing PhysicsBody/PhysicsServer/PhysicsBackendCollider role family without adding production declarations; actual motion, prepared inputs, lifecycle and complete recovery remain shared across selected owners.

[Mass profile publication recovery](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#mass-profile-publication-recovery) uses the existing PhysicsBodyRuntime/PhysicsBody/PhysicsServerCollider/PhysicsSpace checkpoint family. Attachment-qualified failed mass state is prepared before numerical use without adding production types or serialized fields.

[Gravity publication recovery](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#gravity-publication-and-terminal-parameter-guards) retains one failed effective scalar and an in-flight flag in the existing PhysicsColliderBackend attachment facade. Runtime/body/query/checkpoint preparation and scene parameter failure guards reuse existing owners; no public type or serialized field is added.
