[Collider creation operation audit](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#collider-creation-operation-audit) verifies existing BodyCreate/AreaCreate and selected CreateCollider hooks without adding runtime declarations: detached identity, permanent result claims, raw/scene reentry and failed live/whole-tree activation recovery execute through public CPU/software-GPU/registered JIT/NativeAOT consumers with strict zero warmed managed bytes. Typed parameters and monitoring callbacks retain separate Partial rows.

[Area overlap publication operation audit](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#area-overlap-publication-operation-audit) verifies the existing PhysicsAreaRuntime, selected SetAreaMonitoring/GetAreaOverlapCount/ReadAreaOverlaps and PhysicsBackendOverlap family without adding runtime declarations. Both callback lanes, independent monitorability, exact logical compound/custom records, invalid-result preservation, error continuation and lifecycle execute in public CPU/software-GPU/registered JIT/NativeAOT consumers with zero warmed managed bytes; complete numerical/network/native/platform/performance acceptance remains open.

[Finite hierarchy pose and Area transform audit](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#finite-hierarchy-pose-and-area-transform-audit) adds finite global-pose gates in PhysicsBody and Area before numerical creation/preparation, including body basis-cache reuse. Existing scene/raw Area transform operations retain typed authoring and selected geometry across all scene siblings, errors and lifecycle; public CPU/software-GPU/registered JIT/NativeAOT full frames retain zero managed allocation. No runtime declaration is added; full membership/numerical/network/native/platform/performance acceptance stays open.

Embedded Window chrome now consumes all thirteen Window theme items with retained TextLine title/outline layout, real normal/pressed icons, focused/unfocused styles, captured release/cancel and constrained outside resizing. Source: [Window.Decorations.cs](../src/Scene/Main/Window.Decorations.cs), [SceneTree.WindowDecorations.cs](../src/Scene/Main/SceneTree.WindowDecorations.cs), [ThemeDB.Windows.cs](../src/Scene/Resources/ThemeDB.Windows.cs); [contract and verification](components/popup-windows.md#embedded-decoration-theme-and-input). Independent native child hosts retain their own prerequisite.

[Mixed space membership operation audit](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#mixed-space-membership-operation-audit) verifies the existing PhysicsServer/PhysicsSpace/PhysicsBody/PhysicsServerCollider attachment family without adding runtime declarations. Public CPU/no-device, software-GPU and registered JIT/NativeAOT owner/separate consumers preserve scene/raw identity, four body roles, sleeping state, actual target simulation and failure cleanup at zero warmed managed bytes. Cold attachment allocations and broader numerical/network/native/platform/performance acceptance stay separate.

RenderingDevice sparse output mapping executes through [RenderingDevice.FragmentOutputs.cs](../src/Servers/Rendering/Device/RenderingDevice.FragmentOutputs.cs), compact native color targets and logical blend/resolve slots. [Contract and checks](components/local-compute.md#sparse-framebuffer-outputs) cover exact masks, native typed MRT/MSAA/input/canvas pixels and prepared zero-managed-byte draw/readback intervals. No public declaration is added.

[Complete body parameter operations](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#complete-body-parameter-operations) verify the existing PhysicsServer/PhysicsBodyRuntime/PhysicsBody/PhysicsServerCollider ten-pair parameter family without adding production declarations. The shared public physical suite corrects independent-provider damping/order/wake behavior, retains every scene/raw body role and measures zero owner/all-thread warmed managed bytes; full numerical/network/native/platform/performance acceptance remains separate.

Viewport.GUISnapControlsToPixels executes through [Viewport.Rendering.cs](../src/Scene/Main/Viewport.Rendering.cs) stored metadata and [Control.cs](../src/Scene/GUI/Control.cs) visual projection. [Contract/checks](components/canvas-rendering.md#gui-control-pixel-snapping) distinguish visual origins from fractional logical/input coordinates, including native nested/Y-sorted/layer/offset paths.

[Complete body state operations](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#complete-body-state-operations) update PhysicsBody.ServerMode, RigidBody, PhysicsBodyRuntime.State and PhysicsServer state XML. Effective static roles report detached inactivity consistently. RigidBody adds inherited stored sleep descriptors through the internal PropertyDescriptor capture hook, preserving the dynamic request separately from current static inactivity. No production types or public members are added. Public JIT/NativeAOT selected state, recovery, membership and storage checks complete 67/67 profiles with strict zero warmed managed bytes; wider acceptance remains open.

[Complete integration policy operations](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#complete-integration-policy-operations) update the existing PhysicsServer body registry retirement path and callback/direct-state XML. Releasing scene/raw bodies clears registered force/sync delegates and cached views; invalid retained views no longer extend callback-data ownership. Detachment/reentry and explicit local callback history retain their established policies. Public JIT/NativeAOT contact-limit, omission, callback, lifecycle and numerical companions complete 61/61 profiles with strict zero warmed managed bytes. No production type or public member is added; wider acceptance remains open.

[Complete world parameter operations](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#complete-world-parameter-operations) route the existing nine PhysicsServer sleep/contact/iteration/default-constraint getters through the guarded common implementation-input reader. Actual selected numerical hooks can read retained configuration without queries/publication, while ordinary owner/failure rules and write/query guards remain. Public JIT/NativeAOT numerical/read/recovery/lifecycle/copy and root policy companions complete 82/82 distinct profiles with strict zero warmed managed bytes. No production type or public declaration is added; wider acceptance stays open.

[Complete force operations](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#complete-force-operations) retain all thirteen shared scene/raw force capabilities. Scene ConstantForce/ConstantTorque now check terminal-world access before retained zero/equal writes. Public JIT/NativeAOT CPU/no-device, software-GPU and registered owner/separate checks exercise actual role/mass/rotated-center response, lifecycle, guards and strict zero warmed managed bytes; 64/64 distinct profiles include serial root companions. No production type or public declaration is added; wider acceptance stays open.

[Complete space activation operations](components/physics-registered-adapter.md#complete-space-activation-operations) reuse existing PhysicsServer/PhysicsServerBackend/PhysicsSpace ownership and link PhysicsActivityTests into the public-only consumer. Shared selected-world pause/resume, force/spring and callback/statistics behavior now execute on CPU/software-GPU/registered owner/separate JIT and NativeAOT profiles. No production type or runtime declaration is added.

TextServer wrapping and owned substrings use the existing TextLine/TextLayout types; their private source-version leases and logical glyph caches are documented in [the component](components/text-server.md#line-wrapping-and-owned-substrings). No additional public result type is introduced.

FontContourPoint, FontGlyphContours, FontSizeCacheInfo and TextServer.ContourPointTag are the typed copied results of [owned font-cache operations](components/text-server.md#owned-font-sources-and-caches); their source is src/Servers/Text/FontGlyphContours.cs. They expose no native ownership.

[Hollow pair contacts](components/physics-custom-geometry.md#hollow-pair-contacts) now exercise the public-only annulus/annulus format through standalone/selected resource pairs and registered scene/raw queries and numerical response. Both surfaces/orders retain strict warmed zero managed allocation; built-in custom attachment, arbitrary algorithms and full native/network/hardware acceptance remain open.

[Oblique long circle motion](components/physics-queries.md#oblique-long-circle-motion) verifies the existing public cast/overlap and scene/raw body-motion paths across four unit-basis poses, both source/peer roles and signs. CPU/software-GPU/registered owner/separate CLR and NativeAOT profiles retain the existing tolerances and strict zero warmed managed allocation; wider acceptance remains open.

[Factory result ownership](components/scripting.md#factory-result-ownership) protects borrowed physical/script resources and live nodes from invalid Script.New and file-factory results. CLR source-asset checks retain actual CPU/software-GPU/registered simulation and zero warmed managed bytes; NativeAOT source registration and full live-script/duplication acceptance remain open.

[Borrowed resource identity lifetime](components/physics-shape-data.md#borrowed-resource-identity-lifetime) now releases unused Shape sources after collection while actual raw Body/Area slots retain their resources. Owned views and the existing typed Resource.GetRID families retain their owning identities and disposal rules.

[One-way impact admission](components/physics-queries.md#one-way-body-motion-entry) updates existing CPU motion, resident motion-kernel and public provider paths; it introduces no production type or public API.


[Complete body motion operation](components/physics-motion-operation.md) records the selected CPU/resident-GPU/registered geometry, recovery, restored timing, result ownership and failure audit. Its operation row is Implemented; physics is 1304/1369 (95.25%) with 65 declarations open. Broader numerical and overall-goal gates remain open.

[Host identity and runtime features](components/host-platform.md) extend existing OS, ProjectSettingsRegistry, RenderingServer, CanvasBackend/GPUCanvasBackend and NativeSystemFonts implementations. No new production type is introduced; public host queries and session compression observations use their retained owners.

[Retained distance-field mode](components/canvas-rendering.md#retained-distance-field-mode) extends existing RenderingServer, RenderingCanvasItemState, CanvasItem, CanvasMesh, CanvasParticles and GPUCanvasBackend types. No new production type or native dependency is introduced.

ShaderUniform/ShaderProgram and MaterialState carry validated authored numeric defaults and explicit material ownership; existing InstanceUniformState retains prepared set/clear entries. The static RenderingServer default-query family and nullable/empty-RID reset share [the executable default contract](components/shader-materials.md#authored-uniform-defaults). These additions belong to existing inventoried types, with no new runtime type.
